In this regard, this tutorial paper formally introduces Embodied Multimedia as a cross-disciplinary research paradigm that treats multimodal data as the perceptual and communicative substrate spanning the full perception-decision-action loop. To be specific, we present a four-layer unified architecture comprising Data, Communication, Cognitive, and Evaluation layers, and provide a structured review of key enabling technologies within each layer. Furthermore, we identify five frontier application directions where Embodied Multimedia is positioned to serve a foundational role: multimedia communication, physical intelligence, embodied anomaly perception, the metaverse and interactive multimedia, and AI-driven art creation. Open technical challenges and future research directions are discussed to guide the community in this emerging field.

</details>


### [189] [Encore: Infinite Audio-Video Generation with Adaptive Signal Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04249)
*Shaohua Pan,Junbao Chen,Shengyi He,Jingfeng Xue,Wen Tao,Haocheng Feng,Siming Fan,Dongwei Pan,Yi Yang,Wei He,Hang Zhou*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出Encore，一种用于长时同步音视频联合生成的方法，通过分解局部连续性与全局一致性两大挑战，并引入自适应信号路由（ASR）机制来调节各条件信号的影响，实验表明其在生成质量和时间一致性上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频生成方法虽能生成良好同步的短片段，但难以扩展到长时生成；长视频生成方法虽通过分块迭代延长了时长，却完全缺乏音频。长时音视频联合生成需要同时维持视频时间连贯性、音频时间连贯性以及跨模态同步，三者通过不同通道进入模型，难度远超单一任务。

Method: Encore将长时音视频联合生成问题分解为两部分：（1）局部连续性通过迭代生成及显式跨块上下文传播解决；（2）全局一致性通过引入参考音视频信号与偏移位置嵌入实现。此外，提出自适应信号路由（ASR），在自注意力中引入可学习的注意力偏置，在交叉注意力输出上引入可学习的残差缩放，使模型能自适应调节各条件信号的影响。Encore端到端训练，推理时通过在整个去噪过程中以真实模态为条件，支持无限长的音频到视频和视频到音频合成。

Result: 在扩展的VerseBench长音视频评估基准上，Encore在生成质量和时间连贯性方面均显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: Encore通过显式分解局部连续性与全局一致性两大挑战，配合自适应信号路由机制，有效解决了长时同步音视频联合生成难题，并支持无限长度的跨模态合成。

Abstract: Existing audio-video generation methods produce well-synchronized clips but are limited to short durations, while long-video generation methods extend duration through chunk-based iterative synthesis yet lack audio entirely. Generating long audio-video jointly is fundamentally harder than either task alone: each chunk must simultaneously maintain video temporal coherence, audio temporal coherence, and cross-modal synchronization, whose conditioning signals enter the model through different pathways. In this work, we present Encore for long-form synchronized audio-video generation. Our key insight is to factor this challenge into: (1) local continuity handled by iterative generation with explicit cross-chunk context propagation, and (2) global consistency enforced via reference audio-video signals with shifted position embedding. Building on this design, we propose Adaptive Signal Routing (ASR), which introduces learnable attention biases within self-attention and learnable residual scales on cross-attention outputs, enabling the model to adaptively modulate the influence of each conditioning signal. Trained end-to-end for joint audio-video generation, Encore also supports infinite-length audio-to-video and video-to-audio synthesis at inference by conditioning on the ground-truth modality throughout the denoising process. Experiments on our extended VerseBench for long audio-video evaluation demonstrate that Encore significantly outperforms existing methods in both generation quality and temporal coherence. Code and data for this paper are at https://github.com/shaohua-pan/Encore.

</details>


### [190] [AVENUE: Audio-Video EditiNg Understanding and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04253)
*Hayeon Kim,Yoojin Jang,Jaejun Yoo*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出AVENUE基准与评估框架，用于系统评估音视频编辑模型的多模态选择性能力，包含1,291个源片段和7,957条编辑指令，覆盖三种编辑类型与三种编辑范式，揭示现有模型在编辑单模态时常导致另一模态发生意外改变这一开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频编辑基准对编辑类型和模态组合覆盖有限，且评估系统往往是模态盲、样本无关的，难以判断模型是否忠实保留了未指定修改的那一模态内容。

Method: 构建AVENUE基准（1,291个源剪辑、7,957条编辑指令，涵盖音频定向、视频定向和音视频耦合三类编辑，来自VGGSound并经人工验证），并设计样本特定、模态感知的评估框架，为每个样本明确指定预期修改与必须保持不变的内容。在此基础上对联合、顺序、分离三种编辑范式下的代表性模型进行系统分析。

Result: 对三种编辑范式的代表性模型评估发现：无论采用何种范式，现有模型在编辑单一模态时都频繁地导致另一模态发生非预期的改变，这是当前音视频编辑领域的根本性开放挑战。

Conclusion: AVENUE提供了覆盖更广、模态感知更强的基准与评估框架，为推进更可控的音视频编辑模型研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Audio-video (AV) editing aims to modify audio and video content according to a target prompt. Unlike single-modality editing, AV editing requires models to infer a modality-selective edit scope from the prompt alone: determining not only what should change, but also which modality should be preserved. Faithfully evaluating such models therefore requires both (i) benchmarks that span diverse edit types and modality categories, and (ii) evaluation that is itself modality-aware and sample-specific. However, existing AV editing benchmarks provide limited coverage of edit types and modality combinations, while current evaluation systems are often modality-blind and sample-agnostic, making it difficult to assess whether models faithfully preserve the unintended modality. To address these gaps, we introduce AVENUE, Audio-Video EditiNg Understanding and Evaluation, comprising two contributions: (1) a benchmark of 1,291 source clips and 7,957 editing instructions across audio-targeted, video-targeted, and AV-coupled edit types, curated and human-verified from VGGSound; and (2) a sample-specific, modality-aware evaluation framework that specifies, for each sample, both the intended change and the content that must remain intact. We evaluate representative AV editing models spanning three editing paradigms : joint, sequential, and separate, providing the first systematic analysis of modality-selectivity across paradigms. Our findings reveal a fundamental open challenge: when editing one modality, existing models frequently induce unintended changes in the other, regardless of paradigm. AVENUE provides a benchmark and modality-aware evaluation framework to drive progress toward more controllable AV editing models. Our dataset is publicly available on Hugging Face: https://huggingface.co/datasets/AVENUE-dataset/AVENUE.

</details>


### [191] [PRISM-Bench: An Audio-Centric Diagnostic Benchmark for Text-to-Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04867)
*Yuchen Sun,Qian Yang,Jun Wang,Detai Xin,Guoqiao Yu,Guanglu Wan,Qi Jia*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM-Bench，这是首个以音频为中心的文生音视频（T2AV）生成诊断基准。基于900个人工验证样本，从音频类型（语音/音乐/音效）和声源可见性（屏上/屏外）两个维度分解音频评估，并通过35条细粒度标准、4个感知维度进行评测。采用增强版MLLM-as-a-Judge协议（盲测+与参考配对对比），与人类评分器平均一致性超过70%。实验表明前沿模型与开源模型存在显著差距，且当前生成范式过度拟合感知保真度，在复杂接地（grounding）和可控生成任务上表现不足，尤其在音乐生成和同步屏上音频方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的T2AV生成评估低估了音频模态的重要性：要么将音频仅作为视频质量的辅助组件对待，要么在缺乏视听关联性的情况下孤立评估音频，导致难以诊断当前系统在音频生成方面的真实成败。

Method: 构建900个人工验证样本的数据集；沿两个正交轴（音频类型和声源可见性）分解音频评估；定义4个感知维度（视听连贯性、音频质量、音频表现力、指令遵循）共计35条细粒度标准；采用基于盲测、与真实参考进行并排比较的增强MLLM-as-a-Judge协议。

Result: 评测协议与人类评分者平均一致性超过70%；对近期T2AV系统的评估揭示前沿模型与开源模型之间的显著性能差距；证明当前生成范式过度拟合感知保真度，在复杂接地和可控生成任务上遇到困难，尤其是在音乐生成和同步屏上音频方面存在短板。

Conclusion: PRISM-Bench提供了一个音频中心、细粒度、可靠对齐人类判断的T2AV诊断工具，揭示了现有系统在音频生成（尤其是音乐与屏上同步音频）方面的系统性不足，为未来T2AV模型在音频模态上的改进指明了方向。

Abstract: Text-to-audio-video (T2AV) generation has advanced rapidly, but its evaluation still underestimates the audio modality. Existing benchmarks either treat audio as an auxiliary component of video quality or assess it in isolation from audiovisual grounding, making it difficult to diagnose where current systems truly succeed or fail in audio generation. We present PRISM-Bench, the first audio-centric diagnostic benchmark for T2AV generation. Built from a rigorously curated dataset of 900 human-verified samples, PRISM-Bench factorizes audio evaluation along two orthogonal axes: audio type (Speech, Music, and Sound) and sound-source visibility (On-screen vs. Off-screen). It evaluates generated content across four perceptual dimensions (Audio-Visual Coherence, Audio Quality, Audio Expressiveness, and Prompt Following) with 35 fine-grained criteria. To ensure reliable assessment, we adopt an enhanced MLLM-as-a-Judge protocol based on blind, side-by-side comparison against ground-truth references, demonstrating strong alignment (over 70% mean agreement) with human raters. Our evaluation of recent T2AV systems highlights a significant performance gap between frontier and open-source models. Furthermore, we demonstrate that current generation paradigms overfit to perceptual fidelity while struggling with complex grounding and control tasks, particularly in generating music and synchronized On-screen audio.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [Spectral-Target Physical Latent Structuring for JEPA-Style World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04264)
*Penghao Zhu,Salvatore Penachio,Kaustav Mukherjee,Aneesh Jonelagadda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"Fourier辅助头"的训练时辅助监督方法，用于解决潜在世界模型（如LeWorldModel）中的"物理表示懒惰"问题——即潜在状态虽未坍缩但未能捕获关键物理属性，导致动态环境中的规划失败。该方法在训练时对潜在空间施加物理信息结构化约束，无需额外推理成本，可推广到任意环境，在动态环境中显著提升规划成功率并改善数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的潜在世界模型即使通过SIGReg等正则化手段防止了表示坍缩，仍然存在一种新的失败模式——"物理表示懒惰"（physical representation laziness）。在高动态环境中，学习到的潜在状态虽然未坍缩，却无法表征关键物理属性，导致下游规划任务普遍失败。这一问题的发现和解决是本文的核心动机。

Method: 提出一种轻量级的"Fourier辅助头"（Fourier auxiliary head）作为训练时辅助监督手段。该方法在训练过程中对潜在空间施加物理信息化的结构化约束，从而强制潜在状态编码关键物理属性。此方法不增加任何推理时计算成本，并且可泛化到任意环境中。

Result: 实验表明：（1）在基线LeWM表现出物理表示懒惰的动态环境中，辅助头大幅提升了规划成功率；（2）在基线未表现懒惰的其他环境中，也有适度改善；（3）优越的规划性能伴随着潜在空间与关键物理属性之间更高的相关性；（4）在低数据场景下，辅助监督对成功率提升尤为明显。

Conclusion: Fourier辅助头方法能够有效规避潜在世界模型中的物理表示懒惰问题，改善整体规划成功率与数据效率，同时不影响推理成本。这为潜在世界模型在动态环境中的物理结构表示学习提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Latent world models have become increasingly popular as a method to predict and plan in latent space rather than pixel space. Recent architectures, such as LeWorldModel (LeWM), jointly train the encoder and predictor using regularization techniques like SIGReg to prevent representation collapse. Even with such regularization preventing representation collapse, we identify a new world model failure mode of \textit{physical representation laziness}, particularly noted in highly dynamic environments. For these lazy cases, the learned latent states do not collapse but nonetheless fail to represent key physical properties, causing ubiquitous downstream planning failure. To resolve this issue, we propose training-time auxiliary supervision with a lightweight "Fourier auxiliary head", which enforces physically-informed structuring of the latent space with no additional inference-time cost and can be generalized to any environment. Experimentally, we show that the auxiliary head substantially improves planning success rates in dynamic environments where the baseline LeWM exhibits physical representation laziness. It also leads to modest improvements in other environments, even when the baseline does not exhibit physical representation laziness. We further observe superior planning performance being accompanied by higher latent space correlations with key physical properties, indicating both the ability of our method to physically structure latent states and the potential planning-side benefit to the learned representation being physically structured. We also see in low-data regimes, auxiliary supervision is particularly impactful in increasing success rate. These findings support the use of our Fourier auxiliary head method to improve both overall success rate and data efficiency, while avoiding representation laziness in latent world models.

</details>


### [193] [ProToMEx: Rapid, Interpretable Explanations via Structured Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04265)
*Athina Georgara,Adarsh Valoor,Sarvapali D. Ramchurn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProToMEx，一种基于概率主题模型（PTMs）的新型模型无关可解释性框架，通过学习潜在"主题"来揭示分类器决策背后的高层语义结构，超越传统的特征重要性归因，同时提供全局和局部解释，且比SHAP和LIME快约30-40倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后验解释器（如SHAP、LIME）主要聚焦于特征归因，为单个特征分配重要性分数，但难以表达驱动模型决策的复杂组合模式，缺乏对语义结构的解释能力。

Method: ProToMEx利用概率主题模型（PTMs）学习潜在"主题"，每个主题代表一个独立的高层分类原因。框架是模型无关的，能同时生成全局解释（模型整体行为）和局部解释（可分离特定预测中多个共存的原因）。

Result: 实验表明ProToMEx生成的解释在保真度上与SHAP和LIME相当，同时在生成局部解释时的摊销计算成本大幅降低，在标准表格数据集和合成数据集上比SHAP和LIME快约30-40倍。

Conclusion: ProToMEx为可解释性提供了一种新范式，不仅保持了解释质量，还显著提升了计算效率，非常适合实时应用场景。

Abstract: Existing post-hoc explainers for machine learning classifiers primarily focus on feature attribution, assigning importance scores to individual features. While valuable, this approach struggles to articulate the complex, combinatorial patterns that often drive a model's decision-making process. To overcome this limitation, we introduce ProToMEx, a new paradigm for explainability that leverages Probabilistic Topic Models (PTMs). Our model-agnostic framework learns latent ''topics'' that represent distinct, high-level reasons for a classification, moving beyond simple feature importance to reveal underlying semantic structures. ProToMEx naturally provides both global explanations of a model's overall behaviour and local explanations that can disentangle multiple co-existing reasons for a specific prediction. We demonstrate empirically that ProToMEx not only produces explanations of comparable fidelity to popular methods like SHAP and LIME but also drastically reduces the amortised computational cost of generating local explanations, making it highly suitable for real-time applications. Specifically, we show that ProToMEx is ~30-40x faster than SHAP and LIME over standardised tabular datasets and synthetic datasets.

</details>


### [194] [A Data Fusion Framework for Grounding Aerospace Surrogate Model via Experimental Wind-Tunnel Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04267)
*Nitin Nagesh Kulkarni,Dheeraj Vemula,Yin Yu,Peter Lyu,Juan J. Alonso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种实验接地校正框架，利用风洞PSP测量数据来修正CFD训练的气动代理模型（Geotransolver）的预测偏差，在NASA CRM翼身构型上验证了该方法的有效性，无需重新训练代理模型即可显著改善与实验数据的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真CFD训练的深度学习代理模型虽然能精确复现CFD预测结果，但由于CFD与实验观测之间存在系统性差异，其预测保真度受到限制。作者希望在不重新训练大规模代理模型的前提下，利用有限的实验测量数据来弥合这一差距，同时保持代理模型的泛化能力和计算效率。

Method: 该方法包含以下核心步骤：(1) 使用Geotransolver代理模型，基于2,300个高保真CFD仿真（NASA CRM翼身构型，几何变化、马赫数0.70-0.85、攻角0-4度）进行训练；(2) 在空间配准的PSP测量数据（两个自由流马赫数0.70和0.85）上训练一个校正网络，学习代理模型预测与实验测量的表面压力分布之间的系统差异；(3) 校正网络叠加在预训练代理模型之上输出最终预测，且不修改代理模型参数。

Result: 校正后模型在马赫数0.85条件下显著改善了与PSP数据的一致性，特别是在翼面吸力峰值、激波位置及后续压力恢复区域，减小了预测误差幅值以及Cp误差超过0.05的湿润表面占比。在保留的攻角测试中，接地代理模型与测量值的偏差在测量Cp范围的2.3-2.7%以内，并在所有测试状态下优于直接插值方法。

Conclusion: 实验测量可以通过学习CFD到实验的系统性差异来"接地"大规模仿真训练的代理模型，同时保留其泛化能力和计算效率。该框架证明了从有限的实验数据中校正CFD代理模型的可行性，为模拟-实验混合建模提供了新的途径。

Abstract: Aerodynamic surrogate models trained on high-fidelity CFD data reproduce numerical predictions of both scalar outputs and entire fields accurately, yet their predictive fidelity is limited by systematic discrepancies between CFD and experimental observations. We present an experimentally grounded correction framework that adapts a CFD-trained deep learning surrogate using wind-tunnel PSP measurements. A Geotransolver surrogate trained on 2,300 high-fidelity CFD simulations of the NASA CRM wing-body configuration, spanning geometric variation, Mach 0.70-0.85, and angles of attack 0 to 4 degrees, reproduces the CFD integrated aerodynamic forces and pitching moment to R2 > 0.99 but does not match the experimental data. To incorporate experimental information without retraining the surrogate, a correction network is trained on spatially registered PSP measurements at two freestream Mach numbers (0.70 and 0.85) across the same angle-of-attack range, learning the discrepancy between the surrogate-predicted and experimentally measured surface-pressure distributions. At Mach 0.85 the correction substantially improves agreement with PSP, particularly at the wing suction peak, shock location, and subsequent pressure recovery, reducing both the magnitude of the prediction error and the fraction of wetted surface on which it exceeds 0.05 in Cp, and it does so from a limited experimental dataset without modifying the pretrained surrogate parameters. On held-out angles of attack the grounded surrogate agrees with measurement to within 2.3-2.7% of the measured Cp range, and outperforms direct interpolation between the measured conditions at every state tested. Experimental measurements can therefore ground a large-scale simulation-trained surrogate by learning systematic CFD-to-experiment discrepancies while preserving its generalization capability and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [195] [Quantum-Assisted Memory-Efficient Training for Parameter-Intensive Wi-Fi-Based Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04271)
*To Truong An,Jie Zhang,Guolin Yin,Junqing Zhang,Yanjiao Li,Trung Q. Duong,Simon L. Cotton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子辅助的内存高效训练框架（Q-MET），用于Wi-Fi人体活动识别（HAR），通过混合量子-经典神经网络间接生成HAR模型参数，大幅减少可训练参数数量，并整合结构化剪枝以支持资源受限设备上的轻量级推理，实验显示参数减少90%-95%且精度保持或超越经典方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Wi-Fi人体活动识别系统主要依赖深度学习模型，但这些模型在训练和推理阶段都面临计算和内存开销巨大的问题，特别是在训练时需要同时更新数百万参数，导致内存消耗过高，难以在真实世界和资源受限的设备上部署。

Method: 提出Q-MET框架，包含两个关键设计：（1）利用混合量子-经典神经网络间接生成HAR模型的参数，避免直接优化数百万参数，大幅降低可训练参数数量；（2）在训练阶段整合结构化剪枝技术，实现轻量级推理，进一步降低模型在部署时的计算和内存需求。

Result: 实验结果表明，与传统基于反向传播的深度学习训练相比，Q-MET在保持甚至超越经典分类精度的同时，实现了90%到95%的可训练参数减少；通过结构化剪枝实现了75%到85%的模型稀疏度，且分类精度损失低于2%。

Conclusion: 这项工作首次采用量子辅助方法同时解决HAR系统在训练和推理阶段的内存效率问题，为在资源受限设备上部署Wi-Fi人体活动识别系统提供了一条可行的新路径。

Abstract: Wi-Fi-based human activity recognition (HAR) has become an important part of integrated sensing and communications, paving the way for a range of context-aware services. However, most existing Wi-Fi-based HAR systems rely on deep learning (DL) models that are computationally and memory intensive in both training and inference, which poses significant challenges for real-world deployment. Conventional training requires simultaneous updates of millions of parameters, leading to prohibitive memory consumption. In this paper, we propose a novel quantum-assisted memory-efficient training framework (Q-MET) designed to improve efficiency in both training and inference. Q-MET utilizes a hybrid quantum classical neural network to indirectly generate parameters for HAR models, significantly reducing the trainable parameter count compared to direct optimization. To further support the deployment on resource-constrained devices, we integrate structured pruning during the training phase. Experimental results demonstrate that Q-MET achieves a 90% to 95% reduction in trainable parameters compared with conventional backpropagation-based DL training while maintaining or even exceeding classical classification accuracy. Additionally, Q-MET supports lightweight inference through structured pruning, achieving 75% to 85% model sparsity with less than 2% loss in classification accuracy. To the best of our knowledge, this work represents the first quantum-assisted approach to simultaneously tackle memory inefficiencies in both the training and inference stages of HAR systems.

</details>


### [196] [Evaluating Large Language Models for Forced Outage Risk Prediction: Benefits and Comparison to Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04272)
*Christos Petridis,Zoran Obradovic,Mladen Kezunovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究在零样本框架下评估了大语言模型预测配电网恶劣天气导致停电风险的能力，实验基于德克萨斯中部六年的停电记录和高分辨率天气数据，将任务建模为三个预测时域下的二分类严重性分类问题。结果显示监督模型在宏观F1和精确率上优于LLM，但新一代LLM成绩具有竞争力；LLM在可操作推理和地理可扩展性方面具有互补优势，与监督模型结合可能是最佳实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索LLM是否能在无需标注训练数据（零样本）的情况下预测天气相关的配电网强制停电风险，评估其作为传统监督学习替代方案或补充方案的潜力。

Method: 将停电风险预测建模为二分类严重性分类任务，覆盖3小时、6小时、12小时三个预测时域；使用六年的停电记录和高分辨率天气数据（德克萨斯中部某公用事业服务区域）；对四种零样本LLM与两种监督分类器进行基准测试，比较两种输入配置（当前天气观测数据vs.天气预报数据）。

Result: 监督模型在宏观F1和精确率指标上优于LLM；新一代LLM在性能上具有竞争力；LLM在可操作推理和地理可扩展性方面展现互补优势。

Conclusion: 将LLM与监督模型结合使用可能是最佳实践，既能发挥监督模型的高精度优势，又能利用LLM在可操作推理和地理可扩展性方面的独特能力。

Abstract: This study examines the ability of large language models (LLMs) to predict the risk of weather-related forced outages in the distribution grid in a zero-shot framework, without labeled training data. The problem is formulated as a binary severity classification task across three forecast horizons (3h, 6h, 12h), using six years of outage records and high-resolution weather data for a utility service area in central Texas. Four zero-shot LLMs are benchmarked against two supervised classifiers across two input configurations: one using current weather observations and the other using weather forecast data. Results show that supervised models outperform LLMs on macro-F1 and precision, while newer LLM generations achieve competitive scores. Beyond accuracy, LLMs offer complementary strengths in actionable reasoning and geographic scalability, suggesting that combining them with supervised models may be the best practice.

</details>


### [197] [BER-PEF: Unified Human Mobility Predictability Evaluation via Bayes Error Rate Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04292)
*En Xu,Jingtao Ding,Zhiwen Yu,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BER-PEF框架，将贝叶斯错误率估计转化为移动性可预测性估计，提供统一的协议来比较可预测性估计器，无需可观测的ground truth。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类移动可预测性的真实值在真实移动数据中无法直接观测，导致缺乏统一标准来评估不同可预测性估计器的优劣。

Method: BER-PEF框架将符号序列、数值轨迹、上下文特征和学习表示映射到统一的特征-标签空间，引入受控扰动曲线和共享的可预测性参考区间，通过测量估计值在区间下方、上方及全区间的偏差来评估估计器表现。

Result: 在Foursquare NYC/TKY、GeoLife和T-Drive数据集上，多个基于BER的估计器在符号序列和数值轨迹上比现有方法具有更低的参考偏差，且其估计能跟踪扰动下经验预测性能的变化。聚合多个扰动水平的证据比依赖单一未扰动观测更可靠。

Conclusion: BER-PEF为异构移动数据上的可预测性估计器评估提供了统一且可验证的路径，即使缺乏ground-truth可预测性也能进行有效比较与选择。

Abstract: Human mobility predictability concerns the best prediction performance attainable from a given target and input information, but its ground truth is not directly observable on real mobility data. We present BER-PEF, a Bayes-error-rate-based framework that converts BER estimation into mobility predictability estimation and provides a unified protocol for comparing estimators without observable ground truth. The framework maps symbolic sequences, numeric trajectories, contextual features, and learned representations into a common feature--label space, then evaluates estimator outputs along controlled perturbation curves against a shared predictability reference interval by measuring deviations below the interval, above the interval, and across the full interval. Experiments on Foursquare NYC and TKY, GeoLife, and T-Drive show that several BER-based estimators achieve lower reference discrepancy than existing predictability methods on symbolic sequences and numeric trajectories, while their estimates track changes in empirical prediction performance under perturbation. Additional analyses show that contextual inputs and multiple structured representations can be evaluated under the same protocol, and that aggregating evidence across multiple perturbation levels provides a more reliable basis for estimator selection than relying on a single unperturbed observation. BER-PEF therefore offers a unified and verifiable path for evaluating predictability estimators on heterogeneous mobility data when ground-truth predictability is unavailable.

</details>


### [198] [Data-Driven Learning of Unknown Nonlinear Differential Equations Using Functional Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04329)
*Seyyed Shaho Alaviani,Yongzhi Qu,Gregory W. Vogl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的可解释机器学习方法，用于从单一状态轨迹数据中学习非线性常微分方程（ODEs）的未知向量场。该方法基于泛函分析与算子理论，在函数空间中构造积分形式的代价函数（而非离散误差和），并采用增量学习算法在线处理新样本，可同时发现未知外力与潜在动力学，适用于受迫/非受迫、自治/非自治系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的非线性ODE发现方法通常依赖于离散误差和的代价函数，且需要一定的物理先验知识，缺乏对未知力场与时间变化系统的系统性处理能力。作者希望提出一种无需先验物理知识、仅需单一状态轨迹数据即可学习未知向量场的新方法。

Method: 该方法的核心创新在于：(1) 基于泛函分析与算子理论重新构建ODE发现问题的表述；(2) 在函数空间中构造两个函数间的积分距离作为代价函数，替代传统离散误差和；(3) 提出增量学习算法以在线方式处理新增训练样本，从而同时学习未知时间函数的外力和潜在动力学。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法能够在单一状态轨迹数据下有效发现未知向量场，适用于自治/非自治以及受迫/非受迫动力学系统，并能同时识别随时间变化的外部力和系统内部动力学，相较于现有方法展现出优势。

Conclusion: 作者通过将问题重新表述到泛函分析与算子理论框架下，并采用函数空间的积分度量与增量学习策略，成功实现了无需物理先验知识、仅凭单一轨迹即可解释性地发现非线性动力学系统的未知向量场，为可解释机器学习在动力学系统发现领域提供了新的方法论路径。

Abstract: In this paper, the problem of data-driven discovery of nonlinear ordinary differential equations (ODEs) is recast, and a new interpretable machine learning (ML) method is proposed. The proposed method aims to learn the unknown vector field of nonlinear dynamics without prior knowledge of the system's physics from only one single state trajectory's data. The proposed method has two fundamental differences with existing methods: 1) the formulation presented in this method is derived based on Functional Analysis and Operator Theory, and 2) the cost function is constructed in the function space as a distance between two functions as an integral, instead of the discrete-sum of errors used in existing ML approaches. An incremental learning algorithm is proposed to learn the unknown vector field to handle new training samples in an online manner. The proposed method can discover the unknown vector field from both forced and unforced autonomous and non-autonomous (or time-varying) dynamical systems. The proposed method is able to simultaneously discover unknown external forces as a function of time and unknown underlying dynamics. Finally, numerical examples are given to demonstrate the advantages of the proposed method.

</details>


### [199] [Modular Deep Recurrent Neural Network: Application to Quadrotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04339)
*Nima Mohajerin,Steven L. Waslander*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模块化深度递归神经网络（RNN）架构，通过引入前馈层间连接来增强多层RNN对高阶动态和非线性的建模能力，同时缓解空间维度上的梯度消失/爆炸问题，并在四旋翼飞行器高度动态建模中验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RNN架构部署过程繁琐，且多层RNN在空间维度上存在梯度消失/爆炸问题，导致难以有效学习和建模高维非线性动态系统（如四旋翼飞行器）的行为。

Method: 提出一种模块化设计框架以方便部署不同RNN架构并自动计算梯度；在此框架下构建一种包含前馈层间连接（feedforward inter-layer connections）的多层RNN新架构，该架构通过跨层前馈连接改善信息流动，缓解空间维度的梯度问题。

Result: 在四旋翼飞行器高度动态建模的案例中，所提出的网络结构能够成功学习模型，而现有方法无法快速泛化甚至完全无法泛化；前馈层间连接显著提升了多层RNN对高阶动态和非线性动力学建模的能力，并缓解了空间维度的梯度消失/爆炸问题。

Conclusion: 前馈层间连接是改善深度RNN学习能力与训练稳定性的有效手段，模块化设计为探索新颖RNN架构提供了便利，该方案在复杂动态系统建模任务中优于现有方法。

Abstract: A modular deep Recurrent Neural Network (RNN) is introduced to facilitate the process of deploying various architectures of RNNs, and to automatically compute derivatives for gradient-based learning methods. The modularity leads to a set of new architectures, one of which includes feedforward inter-layer connections. By adding feedforward inter-layer connections in a multi-layer RNN, it is observed that the capability of the RNN to learn and model high-order dynamics and nonlinearities is significantly improved. The problem of vanishing/exploding gradient in space for a multilayer RNN is also alleviated using feedforward connections. These results are demonstrated using a quadrotor case study, for which a model of the altitude dynamics is learned with our particular network structure, while existing methods are unable to generalize as quickly or at all.

</details>


### [200] [SharedSAE: One Feature Dictionary Across Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04344)
*Daniil Ognev,Célian Vasson,Lijie Hu,Kentaro Inui,Benjamin Heinzerling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SharedSAE方法，用一个共享稀疏自编码器（SAE）替代多个针对不同语言模型分别训练的SAE。该方法结合共享字典与模型特定的编码器-解码器对，通过仅对选择分数进行归一化来保留激活幅度，并利用模型dropout实现单模型推理。在四个1B规模的不同家族语言模型上训练后，SharedSAE保留了专用SAE 96.6%的平均解释方差，跨模型潜在激活相关性是后对齐独立SAE的1.8倍，且冻结字典后可高效适配新模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAE的可解释性工作需要为每个模型重复进行SAE训练和潜在特征标注，成本高昂且效率低下。作者旨在证明单个共享SAE可以替代一组专门的模型专属SAE，从而减少重复训练和标注的开销。

Method: SharedSAE采用共享字典结合模型特定编码器-解码器对的结构。与先前方法丢弃激活幅度并要求推理时所有模型在场不同，SharedSAE仅对选择分数（selection scores）进行归一化以保留幅度信息，并引入模型dropout机制使单个模型可独立进行推理。

Result: 在四个1B规模、分属不同家族且使用不同分词器的语言模型上，SharedSAE保留了专用SAE 96.6%的平均解释方差；其潜在激活的跨模型相关性是事后对齐的独立SAE的1.8倍；潜在描述可跨模型迁移；词典冻结后新模型可高效适配，重建质量接近专用SAE且无需重新标注。

Conclusion: SharedSAE证明了跨模型共享SAE字典的可行性，大幅减少了多模型可解释性分析中重复训练与标注的成本，同时保持了接近专用SAE的重建质量和良好的特征可迁移性，为高效的多模型神经系统分析提供了新途径。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used to interpret language model activations, but SAE training and latent labelling are typically repeated for every model. Here, we show that a single shared SAE can replace a collection of dedicated per-model SAEs. Our method, SharedSAE, combines a shared dictionary with model-specific encoder-decoder pairs. Unlike the closest prior method, which discards activation magnitudes and requires all models at inference, SharedSAE instead normalizes only selection scores, preserving magnitudes, and uses model dropout for single-model inference. We train SharedSAE on four 1B-scale base language models spanning distinct families and tokenizers. Despite sharing its latents across models, SharedSAE retains 96.6% of dedicated SAEs' mean explained variance; its latent activations exhibit cross-model correlations 1.8 times as high as separate SAEs aligned post-hoc, and its latent descriptions transfer across models. After the dictionary is frozen, new models can be efficiently adapted to it, achieving near-dedicated-SAE reconstruction quality while reusing the shared latent descriptions.

</details>


### [201] [A Quantum Variational Approach to Prototypical Recurrent Unit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04354)
*Mahyar Sadeghi Garjan,Tommaso Cesari,Michel Barbeau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的量子原型循环单元（QPRU），其参数量显著少于经典循环架构（LSTM、GRU）和量子变体（QLSTM、QGRU），同时保持与最先进基线相当的预测性能，并具有更好的可扩展性和更少的可训练参数等优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环神经网络（如LSTM和GRU）及其量子变体（QLSTM和QGRU）参数量较大，导致模型复杂度和计算资源开销较高，需要一种更轻量、更高效的量子循环架构来降低参数量同时保持预测性能。

Method: 提出一种轻量级的量子原型循环单元（QPRU），通过紧凑的结构设计大幅减少可训练参数量，使其参数规模显著低于经典LSTM/GRU以及量子QLSTM/QGRU变体。

Result: QPRU在显著降低参数量的条件下，达到了与最先进基线相当的预测性能，同时展现出更优的可扩展性和更少的可训练参数等结构和实践优势。

Conclusion: QPRU作为一种轻量级量子循环架构，能够在保持竞争性预测性能的同时大幅减少参数量，为提高量子循环模型的效率和可扩展性提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce a lightweight Quantum Prototypical Recurrent Unit (QPRU) that requires significantly fewer parameters than both classical recurrent architectures, such as Long Short- Term Memory (LSTM) and Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU), and quantum variants, including Quantum LSTM (QLSTM) and Quantum GRU (QGRU). Despite its compact design, the QPRU achieves competitive forecasting performance, matching state-of-the-art baselines while offering important structural and practical advantages, including enhanced scalability and a reduced number of trainable parameters.

</details>


### [202] [On the Abundance of Critical Points of the t-SNE Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04379)
*Nakul Haridas,Ryan Murray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了t-SNE算法的能量景观。由于该算法对应的能量函数是非凸的，其行为难以从理论上严格理解。作者针对一族包括原始t-SNE及其大数据极限在内的广义能量函数，对于在特征空间中具有连续对称性的密度分布，通过识别特征空间与目标嵌入空间中成对的离散对称性（这些对称性在梯度动力学下被保持），构造了无限族不同的临界点。这些临界构型表现出拓扑破坏和虚假聚类等经验中常观察到的特征。数值和解析例子贯穿全文以说明该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: t-SNE算法虽被广泛采用，但其能量函数的非凸性使得难以严格理解算法在很多设置下究竟捕捉了什么。已知的大量数值例子表明能量景观复杂，存在许多不尊重底层数据拓扑或聚类结构的局部极小值。本文旨在为这些现象提供首个严格的理论解释步骤。

Method: 针对一族包含原始t-SNE及近期发现的大数据极限的广义能量函数，对于特征空间中满足连续对称性的密度分布，通过识别一对离散对称性（一个在原始特征空间，一个在目标嵌入空间），这些对称性对在梯度动力学下被保持，从而构造出无限族不同的临界点。

Result: 构造了无限族的临界点，这些临界构型展现出拓扑破坏和虚假聚类等经验中常观察到的多种特征。通过数值和解析例子对方法进行了验证和说明。

Conclusion: 该工作为t-SNE复杂能量景观中观测到的非平凡现象（如虚假聚类和拓扑破坏）提供了严格的理论解释的第一步，揭示了这些现象与特征空间和目标空间中离散对称性之间的深层联系。

Abstract: This paper considers the energy landscape of the t-SNE algorithm. While this algorithm has enjoyed broad adoption, the non-convexity of the associated energy has made it difficult to rigorously understand what the algorithm captures in many settings. In particular, a number of well-known numerical examples, several of which are reproduced in this article, suggest a complicated energy landscape with many local minimizers that do not respect the topology or clustering structure of the underlying data. This work seeks to provide first steps towards a rigorous explanation of these phenomena. Specifically, for a general family of energies, which include both the original t-SNE algorithm and recently identified large data limits, and for densities in feature space which obey a continuous symmetry, we construct infinite families of distinct critical points. These critical points are based upon identifying pairs of discrete symmetries, one in the original feature space and the other in the target embedding space, which are preserved under gradient dynamics. These critical configurations exhibit many characteristics, such as topology breaking and spurious clustering, which are often observed empirically. Finally, numerical and analytical examples are given throughout as a means of illustrating the approach.

</details>


### [203] [Disentangling Attention in Deep Operator Learning: A Controlled Study of Data-Driven and Physics-Informed Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04407)
*Amar Alem Koric,Qibang Liu,Seid Koric*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究对五种带有不同注意力机制的DeepONet变体进行了受控的系统性对比实验，在数据驱动和物理信息两种训练模式下，测试于一维非线性扩散-反应方程、一维粘性Burgers方程和二维Poisson热传导问题。结果表明，逐传感器token化结合交叉注意力可将经典DeepONet的相对L2误差降低2.4-28.0倍，最佳注意力配置可达3.5-32.3倍；交叉注意力是最可靠的机制，而分支自注意力仅在大型空间复杂输入时有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经算子能以远超传统数值求解器的速度进行正向求解，但现有研究中注意力机制的引入往往同时改变多个架构组件，难以厘清究竟是哪个组件真正提升了精度。因此作者希望通过受控实验系统地隔离并评估各注意力机制的独立贡献。

Method: 对五种具有不同注意力机制的DeepONet变体进行受控对比研究，分别隔离交叉注意力、自注意力、token化方式和注意力深度的影响，在数据驱动和物理信息两种训练范式下进行系统评估，测试对象涵盖三类偏微分方程基准问题。

Result: 交叉注意力结合逐传感器token化将误差降低2.4-28.0倍，最优配置达到3.5-32.3倍；分支自注意力单独使用时不稳定性明显——对一维问题有害但对复杂的二维源场有帮助，叠加在交叉注意力之上可改善所有六个测试场景；全局预混合无一致收益；增加交叉注意力深度可提升精度但收益递减且在物理信息训练下成本显著上升。

Conclusion: 查询依赖的交叉注意力是最可靠的注意力机制，应作为DeepONet架构改进的首选方案；分支自注意力则更适合处理大规模、空间结构复杂的函数输入场景，两者结合可在所有情况下带来一致的精度提升。

Abstract: Deep neural operators learn mappings between input functions and complete PDE solution fields, enabling forward evaluations of new problem instances orders of magnitude faster than conventional numerical solvers. Attention mechanisms have recently been introduced into neural operators, but most studies change several architectural components at once, making it difficult to identify what actually improves accuracy. This work presents a controlled and systematic study of five deep operator network (DeepONet) variants with distinct attention mechanisms, trained under both data-driven and physics-informed regimes, to isolate the effects of cross-attention, self-attention, tokenization, and attention depth. We evaluate them on a source-driven transient one-dimensional nonlinear diffusion-reaction equation, a transient one-dimensional viscous Burgers equation with variable initial conditions, and a two-dimensional Poisson heat-conduction problem with heterogeneous source fields. Per-sensor tokenization with cross-attention reduces the mean relative L_2 error of the classical DeepONet in all benchmark-training combinations by factors of 2.4-28.0, while the best attention configurations reach 3.5-32.3. Branch self-attention paired only with dot-product fusion is inconsistent, degrading the one-dimensional problems while helping the more complex two-dimensional source field; added on top of cross-attention it improves all six cases, though by less than cross-attention fusion alone. Global pre-mixing provides no consistent benefit. Increasing cross-attention depth further improves accuracy, but with diminishing returns and a substantially higher cost under physics-informed training. Overall, query-dependent cross-attention is the most reliable mechanism, whereas branch self-attention is most useful for large, spatially complex functional inputs.

</details>


### [204] [REFINE: LLM Refinement over Budgeted Text-Attributed Graphs for Personalized Medical Concept Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04415)
*Mohsen Nayebi Kerdabadi,Arya Hadizadeh Moghaddam,Dongjie Wang,Zijun Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REFINE是一个面向患者个性化医疗概念编码的KG感知预算化LLM图细化框架，从全局文本属性知识图谱出发构建患者特定时序图，通过强化学习策略为每个观测代码选择个性化KG扩展预算，并结合异构GNN与冻结LLM的图感知软提示来精炼概念表示，在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗概念编码器对患者采用统一处理方式，忽略了同一个代码在不同患者的临床背景和轨迹下具有不同的含义和预测价值。作者希望利用文本属性知识图谱（TKG）来学习患者个性化的概念表示，但面临两个关键挑战：(1)如何决定每个观测代码应纳入多少KG上下文；(2)如何将语义信息与患者特定关系结构对齐。

Method: REFINE框架包含以下核心组件：从全局TKG构建患者特定的时序图谱；使用顺序强化学习策略为每个观测到的医疗代码选择个性化的KG扩展预算；通过异构GNN处理患者图谱以捕获关系感知的结构依赖；利用冻结的LLM通过图感知软提示对概念表示进行语义精炼。

Result: 在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV数据集上的实验表明，REFINE持续改进多种EHR骨干模型的表现，超越了强基线方法，并且在组件消融、KG选择和低数据量场景下均展现出稳健的性能增益。

Conclusion: REFINE证明了将知识图谱结构信息与LLM语义能力相结合、并通过强化学习实现患者个性化KG扩展预算是有效的医疗概念编码方法，为EHR预测提供了可推广的改进方案。

Abstract: Learning rich medical concept representations is essential for EHR prediction. Text-attributed knowledge graphs (TKGs) provide a natural foundation by organizing heterogeneous medical relations together with textual semantics. However, most existing encoders process concepts uniformly across patients, despite the fact that a code's meaning and predictive value depend on patient-specific clinical context and trajectory. Learning patient-personalized concept representations from TKGs introduces two key challenges: (1) deciding how much KG context to incorporate for each observed code, and (2) aligning semantic information with the patient-specific relational structure. We propose REFINE, a KG-aware budgeted LLM graph refinement framework for patient-personalized medical concept encoding. Starting from a global TKG, REFINE constructs patient-specific temporal graphs. A sequential reinforcement learning policy selects a personalized KG expansion budget for each observed code. The resulting patient graph is processed by a heterogeneous GNN to capture relation-aware structural dependencies, while a frozen LLM uses graph-aware soft prompts to semantically refine concept representations. Experiments on MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV show that REFINE consistently improves diverse EHR backbones, outperforms strong baselines, and demonstrates robust gains across component ablation, KG selection, and data insufficiency.

</details>


### [205] [Beyond a Universal Forecasting Selector: Demand-Conditioned Model Selection across Demand Patterns and Horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04425)
*Adolfo González*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了预测模型选择器是否应作为预测过程中依赖上下文条件的组成部分，比较了五种选择机制（RMSSE、ERA、OWA、CCG-AHSC、CCG-AHSCD）在24个优化预测模型、9个数据集、3种训练-测试划分及1至12个周期视界下的表现，结果表明没有一种选择器在所有条件下占优，需采用依赖上下文的预测模型选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构需求环境下，最适合的预测模型决策规则可能随需求结构、数据可用性和预测视界而变化，但预测模型选择本身是否应被视为依赖上下文的过程尚不清楚。本文旨在探究选择器在不同条件下是否具有情境依赖性。

Method: 对比五种选择机制（RMSSE、ERA、OWA、CCG-AHSC和CCG-AHSCD），在24个优化预测模型、9个数据集、3种训练-测试划分以及1至12个周期的预测视界下进行系统性实验。通过全局相对准确度（GRA）、统计检验以及最佳可实现模型基准从事后角度评估选择器性能。

Result: 没有任何选择器能在所有条件下占优。CCG-AHSC和CCG-AHSCD在平滑需求及若干零散配置下更具竞争力，而OWA和ERA在间歇性和块状需求场景中表现更好。选择器的适用性随历史数据可用性和预测视界变化而变化。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持预测模型选择应采用依赖上下文的方法而非通用的普适性方法，选择器本身应被视为预测过程中的情境依赖组件。

Abstract: Forecasting-model selection remains difficult in heterogeneous demand because the most suitable decision rule may vary with demand structure, data availability, and forecasting horizon. This study examines whether the selector itself should be treated as a context-dependent component of the forecasting process. Five selection mechanisms - RMSSE, ERA, OWA, CCG-AHSC, and CCG-AHSCD - are compared across 24 optimized forecasting models, nine datasets, three training-testing partitions, and horizons from 1 to 12 cycles. Selector performance is evaluated ex post using Global Relative Accuracy (GRA), statistical tests, and a best-attainable-model reference. No selector dominates across all conditions. CCG-AHSC and CCG-AHSCD are more competitive for Smooth demand and several Erratic configurations, whereas OWA and ERA perform better in Intermittent and Lumpy settings. Selector suitability also changes with historical data availability and horizon, supporting a context-dependent rather than universal approach to forecasting-model selection.

</details>


### [206] [A Repeated-Measurement Study for Cultural Analytics of English Song Lyrics Using Five Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04428)
*E. Cho Smith,Samuel Ho,Dawn Laux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了五种大语言模型作为零样本标注器，对英文歌词中四个社会构念（自尊、自我控制、归属寻求、认可寻求）的标注可靠性，发现不同构念的可靠性差异显著，其中自尊的重复测量信度最强，认可寻求最不稳定。研究建议在使用LLM标注进行文化分析前，应报告重复测量稳定性和跨模型一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被用于大规模文化文本标注，但其输出能否作为潜在社会构念的可靠测量尚需验证。在将LLM标注结果视为可扩展的测量工具之前，必须建立其可靠性。

Method: 对大规模歌词语料进行重复标注，评估五种LLM作为零样本标注器的三项属性：（1）跨重复运行的内部一致性；（2）跨模型的收敛性；（3）共识标签向监督分类的可迁移性。研究对象为四个社会构念：自尊、自我控制、归属寻求和认可寻求。

Result: LLM测量在不同构念间并非统一可靠：自尊在多种模型上表现出最强的重复测量信度；认可寻求整体最不稳定；自我控制和归属寻求居中但可靠性依赖具体模型。下游分类显示共识标签包含可学习信号，但可迁移性本身并不能确立构念效度。

Conclusion: LLM标注在文化分析中不应被无条件视为可扩展的测量工具。文献建议在将LLM标注结果用作测量之前，必须报告重复测量稳定性和跨模型一致性等可靠性指标。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to annotate cultural texts at scales that are impractical for human coders. However, before their outputs are treated as measurements of latent social constructs, it is necessary to establish whether those measurements are reliable. This study evaluates five LLMs as zero-shot annotators of four social constructs expressed in English song lyrics: self-esteem, self-control, seeking belonging, and seeking recognition. Using repeated annotations of a large lyric corpus, we examine three properties of LLM-based measurement: consistency across repeated runs, convergence across models, and transferability of consensus labels to supervised classification. The findings show that LLM-based measurement is not uniformly reliable across constructs. Self-esteem exhibits the strongest repeated-measurement reliability across models, while seeking recognition is generally less stable; self-control and seeking belonging show intermediate but model-dependent reliability. Downstream classification further indicates that consensus LLM labels contain learnable signal, although transferability does not itself establish construct validity. Repeated-measurement stability and cross-model convergence should therefore be reported before LLM annotations are treated as scalable measurements in cultural analytics.

</details>


### [207] [Conformity Breaks Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04445)
*Yibo Hu,Hanyu Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示多智能体LLM系统中一个被忽视的安全隐患：一致性证书（conformal certificate）在模型单独作答时有效，但当模型看到其他智能体一致断言错误答案时（即"peer pressure"），其评分机制发生偏移，导致校准失效。实验显示在标准alpha=0.10下覆盖率从90%骤降到74%，而攻击者针对低置信度子群可将近乎减半覆盖率（从87%到47%）。标准一致性修复方法无效，因为问题分布未变，变化的是模型的评分行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体LLM系统中的不确定性量化通常假设模型在单独作答时的校准性能可以迁移到有peer影响的场景。本文指出这一假设不成立：当LLM面对一致给出错误答案的同侪时，其评分逻辑发生"机制偏移"（score-mechanism shift），使原本有效的一致性预测证书失效，存在被攻击者利用的安全漏洞。

Method: 作者提出"score-mechanism shift"概念，将与干净环境校准的模型应用于存在一致错误peer的多智能体场景。通过多个开源权重模型和多选题QA任务进行实验，对比模型单独作答与在有同侪一致错误答案压力下的覆盖表现，并进一步分析攻击者针对低置信度项的子群攻击策略及其对决策层（如是否升级或行动）的影响。

Result: 在标准alpha=0.10运行点下，覆盖率从校准的90%降至74%。平均结果掩盖更严重的失败：攻击者针对证书仍覆盖的低置信度样本进行攻击，该子群覆盖率从87%跌至47%，而受监控的平均值仍看似较高。该失效传播到决策层，系统在应当升级（escalate）时反而因过度置信而采信攻击者的错误答案。标准一致性修复无法解决，因为问题分布未变，改变的是模型的评分机制。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM系统中peer信息会改变模型内在评分机制，使仅基于干净环境校准的一致性证书失效且难以通过标准修复方法弥补。这表明在设计多智能体LLM系统的不确定性保障时，必须将peer影响纳入校准考量，否则存在被恶意智能体操纵而引发决策风险的漏洞。

Abstract: A conformal certificate can be valid when an LLM answers alone and invalid when the same LLM sees peers that unanimously assert a wrong answer. The question is unchanged; the model's score for the correct answer changes. We call this a score-mechanism shift: clean calibration certifies how the model scores answers alone, but not how it scores them under peer pressure. We show that this shift silently breaks conformal prediction in multi-agent LLM systems. Across open-weight models and multiple-choice QA tasks, coverage falls from a calibrated 90% to 74% under unanimous-wrong peers at the standard alpha = 0.10 operating point. The average hides a sharper failure: by targeting the low-confidence items the certificate still covers, an attacker nearly halves coverage on that subgroup, from 87% to 47%, while the monitored average remains much higher. The failure also reaches the decision layer: a system that should escalate when uncertain can instead become confident enough to act on the attacker's wrong answer. Standard conformal fixes do not solve the problem, because the question distribution has not changed; the model's scoring behavior has.

</details>


### [208] [When Load-Balancing Goes Too Far: Expert Pruning in Over-Dispersed Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04453)
*Berkcan Kapusuzoglu,Connor Pryor,Sangwoo Cho,Supriyo Chakraborty,Shi-Xiong Zhang,Sambit Sahu,Milind Naphade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了过度分散路由下Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）模型的专家剪枝问题。作者发现，在剧烈负载均衡训练导致的过度分散路由场景中，路由器概率作为重要性信号会失效，困惑度无法预测下游任务精度。为此提出MESA（Minimax Expert Score Allocation）方法，通过迭代提升当前受影响最严重领域专家的得分，最小化最差领域退化而非平均精度，在25%剪枝率下优于激活感知基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有专家剪枝方法依赖路由器概率作为专家重要性信号，但这一假设在过度分散路由（over-dispersed routing）下会失效——由于训练时激进的负载均衡导致token近乎均匀分布在专家之间，重要性信号坍塌。作者观察到该场景下困惑度与下游精度脱节（低困惑度配置反而损害数学推理），且不同评分指标存在领域能力权衡。因此需要理解这一特殊剪枝场景并提出领域感知的剪枝方法。

Method: 提出Minimax Expert Score Allocation（MESA），一种领域感知方法：迭代地识别当前受剪枝影响最严重的领域，并提升服务于该领域专家的重要性分数，以最小化最差领域退化为目标而非平均精度损失。实验涵盖gpt-oss-20B/120B、Gemma-4-26B-A4B和OLMoE-1B-7B等多个模型规模。

Result: 在25%专家剪枝下，MESA实现了最小的跨领域最差退化，在11个基准中的7个上优于激活感知基线，同时降低内存占用；并泛化到gpt-oss-120B、Gemma-4-26B-A4B和OLMoE-1B-7B。发现激活感知评分虽保留数学推理但使GPQA下降18个点，而频率评分则恰好相反。在标准路由（如Mixtral-8x7B-Instruct）下困惑度与精度同步下降，该问题不出现。

Conclusion: 过度分散路由构成一种定性不同的剪枝场景，标准假设在此场景下失效。识别该场景是进行负载均衡MoE模型原则性专家剪枝的前提。MESA通过优化最差领域退化而非平均精度，为该场景提供了有效的领域感知剪枝方案。

Abstract: Expert pruning reduces the memory and serving cost of Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models by removing low-importance experts identified by the router, assuming router probabilities provide a reliable importance signal. We observe that this assumption breaks down under over-dispersed routing, a regime associated with aggressive load-balancing during training, in which tokens are distributed nearly uniformly across experts and importance signals collapse. In this regime, perplexity does not predict downstream task accuracy: on gpt-oss-20B, the lowest-perplexity pruning configuration yields the worst mathematical reasoning, while the highest-perplexity configuration preserves it. This does not occur under standard routing (e.g., Mixtral-8x7B-Instruct), where perplexity and accuracy degrade together. Pruning under over-dispersed routing also exposes a capability trade-off in which no single scoring metric dominates: activation-aware scoring preserves mathematical reasoning but severely degrades knowledge-intensive science (an 18-point gap on GPQA), whereas frequency-based scoring exhibits the reverse. We propose Minimax Expert Score Allocation (MESA), a domain-aware method that iteratively boosts importance scores for experts serving whichever domain is currently worst-affected, minimizing worst-case domain degradation rather than average accuracy. At 25% expert pruning MESA achieves the smallest worst-case degradation across domains, outperforming activation-aware baselines on 7 of 11 benchmarks at a correspondingly reduced memory footprint, and it generalizes to gpt-oss-120B, Gemma-4-26B-A4B, and OLMoE-1B-7B. Our results indicate that over-dispersed routing is a qualitatively distinct pruning regime in which standard assumptions fail, and that recognizing it is a prerequisite for principled expert pruning of load-balanced MoE models.

</details>


### [209] [On-board ML for Trace Gas detection in Imaging Spectroscopy data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04458)
*Vít Růžička,Adam Chlus,Andrew Thorpe,David R. Thompson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在2026年3月东京野外活动中，利用搭载于AVIRIS-5传感器上的边缘机器学习模型实现成像光谱数据的机载实时处理，实现了甲烷点源排放的首次机载检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统成像光谱数据处理依赖地面慢速处理流程，导致瞬态事件（如痕量气体排放）从检测到信息传达的时间延迟过长，无法支持即时后续行动。

Method: 在通信带宽受限的情况下，采用高效且小型的机器学习模型在机载平台直接处理成像光谱数据，仅下传由模型预测出的潜在事件信息，而非完整的datacube。

Result: 首次证明了使用成像光谱数据和边缘机器学习技术在机载条件下成功实现甲烷点源排放的实时检测。

Conclusion: 基于边缘计算的机载处理方案可显著缩短瞬态事件的检测到信息时间，为遥感应急响应和后续行动提供实时支持。

Abstract: Data collected during aerial and spaceborne imaging spectroscopy campaigns enables the detection of transient events such as trace gas emissions. However, current processing pipelines depend on slow, on-the-ground processing, which delays the time to information of each detected event and prohibits immediate follow-up actions. During the Tokyo Field Campaign of March 2026, we explored on-board processing of Imaging Spectroscopy data from the equipped AVIRIS-5 sensor. Due to communication bottlenecks, full datacubes cannot be downlinked immediately during the flight. Instead we downlink the potential events predicted by our efficient and small machine learning model. We show the first on-board detection of methane point source emission with Imaging Spectroscopy data using Edge ML.

</details>


### [210] [Nested Inductive Bias Framework for SPD Manifold Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04466)
*Tushar Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个嵌套归纳偏置框架，通过两阶段微分同胚组合将非欧几里得目标几何拉回到SPD流形上，使深度流形网络同时满足矩阵约束和数据的潜在关系几何，并进一步提出Rational Conformal Metric (RCM)以增强标准向量化架构对离群点的几何鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SPD流形表示学习通常依赖拉回欧几里得度量（如Log-Euclidean Metric），虽然计算高效且能避免域边界越界，但会产生平坦几何，无法捕捉数据固有的关系先验。而像庞加莱度量这类能诱导域对齐关系先验的度量，难以从标准向量表示推广到SPD流形，因此存在一个关键缺口。

Method: 引入嵌套归纳偏置框架，采用两阶段微分同胚组合，将非欧几里得目标几何正式拉回到SPD流形上；基于此构建曲率对齐的黎曼分类器，同时尊重矩阵约束与数据的潜在关系几何；另外提出Rational Conformal Metric (RCM)，通过约束表示空间来建立对离群点的几何鲁棒性。

Result: 在运动学和信号处理基准以及合成实验上验证了方法的有效性。实验表明：当度量曲率与固有数据分布不对齐时，深度流形网络的类别可分性会下降；RCM在标准向量化架构上实现了对离群点的最先进几何鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 度量曲率与数据内在分布的匹配对于深度流形网络的类别可分性至关重要，嵌套归纳偏置框架能够桥接SPD流形上的矩阵约束与关系先验学习，而RCM为向量化架构提供了鲁棒的几何选择。

Abstract: In Geometric Deep Learning, inductive biases serve two primary functions: enforcing manifold constraints and embedding relational priors. Currently, representation learning on SPD manifolds frequently relies on pullback Euclidean metrics, such as the Log-Euclidean Metric, to satisfy the former. While computationally efficient in avoiding domain boundary violations, these metrics induce a flat geometry that may fail to capture the intrinsic relational priors of datasets. While metrics such as the Poincaré metric are widely utilized to induce domain-aligned relational priors, generalizing them from standard vector representations to the SPD manifold has remained a challenge. To bridge this gap, we introduce a Nested Inductive Bias framework that utilizes a two-stage diffeomorphic composition to formally pull back non-Euclidean target geometries onto the SPD manifold. This framework enables the construction of curvature-aligned Riemannian classifiers that simultaneously respect matrix constraints and the latent relational geometry of the data. Empirical evaluations on kinematic and signal processing benchmarks, together with synthetic experiments, demonstrate that deep manifold networks experience degradation in class separability unless the metric curvature aligns with the intrinsic data distribution. Furthermore, for standard vectorized architectures, we propose the Rational Conformal Metric (RCM), designed to establish state-of-the-art geometric robustness against outliers by bounding the representation space.

</details>


### [211] [Hakken: Predicting future discoveries to fill the gaps in today's knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04494)
*Tarek R. Besold,Uchenna Akujuobi,Pablo Sanchez,Alessandra Toniato,Kana Maruyama,Jihun Choi,Samy Badreddine,Frederick Gifford,Daniel Evans-Yamamoto,Sucheendra K. Palaniappan,Miquel Ferrer,Kae Nagano,Iris Rossell,Tom Joy,Hatem ElShazly,Chrysa Iliopoulou,Christoph Wehner,Thiviyan Thanapalasingam,Susana Nunes,Pedro G. Cotovio,Peter Wurman,Peter Stone,Hiroaki Kitano,Michael Spranger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍Hakken，一个领域无关的知识预测与解释系统，利用基于Transformer的预测模型结合时序知识图谱与LLM语义知识，预测科学概念间尚未被记录的关联关系，并通过模型无关解释框架提供评估信息。在生物医学领域的应用中，Hakken建立了时间感知多标签关系预测的新基准，在衰老相关的150万条假设中筛选出高质量预测，其中两条关于TP53-BAMBI和RAF1-TNF的相互作用（具有药物发现与再利用潜力）已获湿实验验证确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统科学知识发现受限于已有知识的演绎推理，缺乏真正的知识预测能力——即建立超出已有知识演绎闭包的全新关联。本文旨在构建一个领域无关的系统，不仅能够预测科学概念间尚未记载的新关系，还能为每项预测提供可解释的信息，帮助科学家评估新关系的合理性。

Method: Hakken结合了（1）基于Transformer的预测模型，构建在大规模研究文献中提取的时序知识图谱序列上；（2）LLM的语义知识融合；（3）模型无关的解释框架，为每个预测提供配套的解释信息。该系统被应用于生物医学领域进行演示验证。

Result: 在生物医学应用中，Hakken的时间感知多标签关系预测达到了新基准水平，在历史数据上长时间跨度内保持输出一致性和信息性。系统对150万条与衰老相关的置信度阈值以上的假设进行评分，经生物学家定性验证后，推进了其中三条进行湿实验验证。两条具有药物发现和再利用潜在影响的预测被实验确认：TP53与BAMBI、RAF1与TNF之间此前未被记录的相互作用。

Conclusion: Hakken证明了基于时序知识图谱与LLM融合的AI系统能够进行有意义的前沿科学知识预测，其预测不仅可解释、可验证，还能在真实实验室环境中产生具有实际科学价值的发现，尤其在药物发现和再利用领域展现出转化潜力。

Abstract: We present Hakken, a domain-agnostic prediction and explanation system performing knowledge prediction, i.e., growing scientific knowledge by establishing novel relationships, ones that are not limited to the deductive hull of previous knowledge. Hakken uses a transformer-based prediction model built on temporal sequences of knowledge graphs extracted from vast bodies of research publications, fused with an LLM's semantic knowledge, to predict the presence and define the type of as-yet undocumented relationships between scientific concepts. It then calls a model-agnostic explanation framework to provide accompanying information for each prediction that allows scientists to evaluate the suggested new relationship. While general purpose, we demonstrate Hakken's practical capabilities by applying it to the biomedical domain. There, Hakken's prediction model establishes a new benchmark for time-aware multi-label relation prediction, and we show that the model's output stays coherent and informative over extended time spans in historic data. In addition, we scored 1.5 million above-confidence-threshold hypotheses related to aging, qualitatively validated batches of these predictions with biologists and progressed three of them for empirical validation in wet-lab. Two predictions with potentially significant impact in the context of drug discovery and repurposing were confirmed, introducing previously undocumented interactions between TP53 and BAMBI, and between RAF1 and TNF, to biomedical science.

</details>


### [212] [An Energy-Based Conservative-Dissipative Latent Neural Evolution Operator for Magnetization Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04530)
*Sebastian Schaffer,Lukas Exl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于微磁磁化动力学仿真的能量基降阶模型，将卷积自编码器与结构化潜在神经常微分方程耦合，利用Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert方程的进动-耗散结构，通过学习得到的标量势能的梯度生成潜在向量场。模型无需时间导数监督或物理能量标签，在推理时仅需一次编码、潜在空间演化，大幅降低轨迹预测成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微磁模拟中，完整的micromagnetic求解器进行长时程轨迹预测计算代价高昂，需要开发降阶模型以降低计算成本，同时保留磁化动力学中进动-耗散这一核心物理结构的精度。

Method: 将卷积自编码器与结构化潜在神经常微分方程耦合：潜在向量场由学习得到的标量势能通过反对称算子（进动项）和对称半正定耗散算子生成；编码器、解码器、潜在能量和算子以短轨迹窗口联合训练，仅使用潜在和重构滚动损失，无需时间导数监督或物理能量标签。

Result: 在NIST μMAG标准问题4的两个外加场方向数据集上，反对称-耗散模型相比仅耗散模型在无中断轨迹预测上精度显著更高；深度-二次势能组合在两个场方向上都取得最佳整体精度，并且在将轨迹延长至两倍训练时域时误差增长更慢。

Conclusion: 融入Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert方程进动-耗散结构的降阶模型能够在不依赖物理能量标签和额外监督的情况下，实现高精度、低成本的长时程微磁轨迹预测，其中深度-二次能量表达与反对称-耗散结构组合最优。

Abstract: We develop an energy-based reduced-order model for micromagnetic magnetization dynamics that couples a convolutional autoencoder to a structured latent neural ordinary differential equation. Motivated by the precessional-dissipative structure of the Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert equation, the latent vector field is generated from the gradient of a learned scalar potential through an antisymmetric operator and a symmetric positive-semidefinite dissipative operator. This potential is learned in nonunique latent coordinates and is not identified with the Gibbs free energy, but decreases monotonically along autonomous continuous-time solutions, while the antisymmetric component permits motion along its level sets. The encoder, decoder, latent energy, and operators are trained jointly on short trajectory windows using latent and decoded-rollout losses alone, without time-derivative supervision, physical-energy labels, or dissipation penalties. At inference, an initial state is encoded once, evolved in latent space, and decoded only at the requested output times, enabling substantially cheaper trajectory prediction than the micromagnetic solver used to generate the training data. We compare quadratic, deep, and additive deep-quadratic latent energies on two datasets parameterized by field amplitude and generated for the two applied-field directions of the NIST $μ$MAG Standard Problem 4. Dissipative-only and antisymmetric-dissipative models achieve comparable accuracy on short training-style windows but differ substantially on uninterrupted rollouts, for which the antisymmetric-dissipative models provide markedly more accurate trajectory predictions. The deep-quadratic energy gives the best overall accuracy for both field directions and exhibits slower error growth when rollouts are extended to twice the training horizon.

</details>


### [213] [Distilled Continuous Diffusion Language Models Can Write Code in Few Steps---or One](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04531)
*Fred Zhangzhi Peng,Kaiwen Zheng,Anru R. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PlaidQ，一个0.7B参数的连续扩散语言模型，用于代码生成。通过分布匹配蒸馏实现几步生成，通过配对轨迹监督实现单步生成。实验表明，16步学生模型在HumanEval和MBPP+上超越512步采样的教师模型；单步即可生成功能正确的程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言生成普遍依赖自回归序列生成或扩散模型的迭代精炼，前者受限于token级串行瓶颈，后者路径过长。作者希望探索连续扩散语言模型能否通过积极蒸馏实现少步甚至单步的高效代码生成，从而突破质量-计算权衡的现有边界。

Method: 将预训练的自回归模型改造为双向连续token嵌入去噪器，构建PlaidQ连续扩散语言模型。采用两种蒸馏策略：分布匹配蒸馏用于少步生成，配对轨迹监督用于单步生成。两者均在连续扩散框架下进行。

Result: 在同等模型规模下，PlaidQ在代码生成上与离散扩散语言模型具有竞争力。蒸馏后，16步学生模型在HumanEval和MBPP+上分别达到31.78和40.49的pass@10，超越512步采样的相同PlaidQ教师模型。配对轨迹蒸馏在单步条件下实现HumanEval的7.07 pass@1，能生成功能正确的程序。

Conclusion: 连续扩散为高效代码生成提供了一条可行的少步乃至单步路径。其意义不仅在于作为语言的另一种表示形式，更在于提供了使语言模型继承连续扩散建模中加速与蒸馏机制的接口。代码与模型已开源。

Abstract: Language generation is almost universally treated as a sequential process: autoregressive models emit one token at a time, while diffusion language models replace token-level seriality with a long trajectory of iterative refinement. In this work, we introduce PlaidQ, a 0.7B continuous diffusion language model for code generation, and show that its trajectory can be aggressively distilled into only a few denoising steps---or even one, enabling efficient code generation. PlaidQ repurposes a pretrained autoregressive model as a bidirectional denoiser over continuous token embeddings. We distill PlaidQ with distribution matching for few-step generation and paired-trajectory supervision for one-step generation. At matched model scale, PlaidQ is competitive with discrete diffusion language models on code generation. Distillation then shifts the quality--compute frontier: a 16-step student reaches 31.78 and 40.49 pass@10 on HumanEval and MBPP+, surpassing the same PlaidQ teacher sampled for 512 steps. At the extreme, paired-trajectory distillation achieves 7.07 pass@1 on HumanEval with a single denoising step, producing functionally correct programs. Together, these results establish continuous diffusion as a viable path to few-step and one-step code generation. Broadly, continuous diffusion is not merely another representation for language: it provides an interface through which language models can inherit the acceleration and distillation machinery of continuous diffusion modeling. Training and inference code and model checkpoints are available at https://github.com/pengzhangzhi/plaidq.

</details>


### [214] [Mitra-v2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04540)
*Yefan Tao,Xiyuan Zhang,Xinyi Liu,Boran Han,Danielle Maddix,Haoyang Fang,Zhen Han,Jiading Gai,Xuanqing Liu,Michael Bohlke-Schneider,Yuyang,Wang,Gerald Friedland,Kevan Mah,Chris Lee,Chris Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Mitra-v2，一个仅在合成数据上训练的表格基础模型，在300多个真实世界数据集上达到最先进的分类和回归性能。Mitra-v2以77M参数（仅为1.6B参数TabFM模型的5%）达到前沿性能水平，在TabArena和TALENT基准上显著超越TabPFN-3等现有模型，并已以Apache-2.0许可证开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据在现实应用中广泛存在（信贷风险、临床预测、设备故障检测和房价估算等），但相比语言和视觉领域的模型，表格基础模型的性能仍有很大提升空间。现有方法在性能、模型规模和泛化能力之间存在权衡，需要在真实世界多样化的表格任务上实现更强的通用性能。

Method: Mitra-v2基于小型2D Transformer骨干架构，仅在合成数据上训练，其预训练分布相比v1版本更大更多样化。该模型支持更长的上下文和更大的特征空间，并通过优化的训练策略从更大的任务分布中有效学习。

Result: 在TabArena全量基准上，Mitra-v2达到与业界规模TabFM和EXAONE Tabular同级的最先进性能，同时在分类和回归任务上大幅超越TabPFN-3。仅用77M参数（TabFM的5%规模）即可匹敌1.6B参数的TabFM。在TALENT基准上表现领先，超越TabPFN-3和TabICLv2，并在超过十类的分类任务中排名第一，尽管预训练仅在最多十类的任务上完成。

Conclusion: Mitra-v2是目前发布的最强、适用范围最广的开源表格基础模型之一，以极小的模型规模实现前沿性能，并以Apache-2.0许可证开放模型权重、推理和微调代码及评估结果。

Abstract: We introduce Mitra-v2, a tabular foundation model that delivers state-of-the-art performance on real-world classification and regression problems, from credit-risk scoring and clinical prediction to equipment-failure detection and house-price estimation. Mitra-v2 is trained only on synthetic data, with a pretraining distribution that is much larger and more diverse than Mitra-v1's. Built on a small 2D Transformer backbone, Mitra-v2 supports longer contexts and larger feature spaces. Improved optimization lets it learn from this larger task distribution. We evaluate Mitra-v2 on the TabArena and TALENT benchmarks, comprising more than 300 real-world datasets under two evaluation protocols. On the full TabArena benchmark, Mitra-v2 delivers state-of-the-art performance at the level of the industry-scale TabFM and EXAONE Tabular models, while surpassing TabPFN-3 by a wide margin in both classification and regression. Mitra-v2 matches the 1.6B-parameter TabFM with only 5% of its size (77M parameters), delivering frontier performance at a fraction of the cost. On TALENT, Mitra-v2 remains among the leading models, clearly outperforming TabPFN-3 and TabICLv2. It also ranks first on classification tasks with more than ten classes, even though it was pretrained only on tasks with at most ten classes. These results make Mitra-v2 one of the strongest and most broadly applicable open tabular foundation models released to date. We release the model weights, the inference and fine-tuning code, and our evaluation results under the Apache-2.0 license.

</details>


### [215] [Fast Surrogate Modeling of Excitable and Oscillatory FitzHugh-Nagumo Dynamics with Parametric Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04549)
*Andrew Franck,Justin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出利用参数条件化的傅里叶神经算子（FNO）作为FitzHugh-Nagumo（FHN）系统的快速可微替代模型，通过FiLM机制按参数向量调节每个傅里叶层，分别在振荡（强直放电）和可激发（动作电位传播）两个分支区域各训练一个算子。振荡区域替代模型达到了亚0.1%的相对L²误差，速度比有限差分基线快近三个数量级；可激发区域则能准确复现放电阈值和传导速度定律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FHN系统作为神经元电压动力学的简化模型，其5维生理参数空间的探索对神经调控和将电压记录映射回生物物理参数至关重要，但经典有限差分求解器使得快速参数扫描代价高昂，因此需要高效的替代模型来加速参数空间的探索。

Method: 采用参数条件化的傅里叶神经算子（FNO）作为FHN电压场和恢复场的一维空间替代模型，通过特征级线性调制（FiLM）将参数向量λ=(D_u, D_v, a, b, τ)注入每个傅里叶层。利用单一分岔分析划分振荡和可激发两种不同模态区域，并在每个区域内分别训练一个算子。

Result: 在振荡区域，替代模型在两个场上均达到亚0.1%的相对L²误差，运行速度比有限差分基线快近三个数量级，跨参数空间均匀泛化，且在训练范围外外推时的误差仅为低个位数百分比。在可激发区域，算子准确复现了放电阈值和c∝√D_u的传导速度定律，并完整再现了行进脉冲，完全捕捉了可激发分岔结构。

Conclusion: 参数条件化的FNO替代模型能够以极高精度和显著加速效率覆盖FHN系统的两个截然不同的动力学区域，不仅实现了快速参数扫描，还保持了物理上的分岔结构忠实性，为神经科学的参数映射和神经调控研究提供了有力的计算工具。

Abstract: The FitzHugh-Nagumo (FHN) system serves as a simplified model of neuronal voltage dynamics, capturing the activator-inhibitor structure behind both isolated action potentials and the rhythmic spiking seen across the brain. Exploring its 5D physiological parameter space is important for neuromodulation and mapping voltage recordings back to biophysics, yet classical finite-difference solvers make rapid parameter sweeps expensive. We train parameter-conditioned Fourier Neural Operators (FNOs) as fast, differentiable surrogates for the FHN voltage and recovery fields on a one-dimensional spatial domain, conditioning each Fourier layer on the parameter vector $λ= (D_u, D_v, a, b, τ)$ via feature-wise linear modulation (FiLM). We apply a single bifurcation analysis that delimits the two distinct regimes the model spans, oscillatory (tonic firing) and excitable (action-potential propagation), and we train one operator in each. In the oscillatory regime the surrogate attains sub-$0.1\%$ relative $L^2$ error on both fields, runs nearly three orders of magnitude faster than the finite-difference baseline, generalizes uniformly across the parameter space, and extrapolates to low single-digit percentage errors outside of the training bounds. In the excitable regime the same operator accurately reproduces the firing threshold and the $c \propto \sqrt{D_u}$ conduction-velocity law and replicates full traveling pulses, fully capturing the excitable bifurcation structure rather than just smoothly interpolating fields.

</details>


### [216] [Training-Free Halving of Activated Experts in Fine-Grained Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04575)
*Xing Chen,Hengshuai Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现MoE模型中router概率的重新归一化会隐式地校准专家输出增益到训练时的top-k值。通过引入一个无参数、零开销的整数k₂（激活top k₁个专家但用top k₂的专家概率质量做归一化），可以大幅缓解推理时减少专家数量带来的性能下降。在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B上，将8个专家减至4个，标准归一化导致4.65点MMLU下降，而k₂=16时仅下降0.35点，同时计算量减半；在更大的Qwen3.5-397B-A17B上结果同样成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细粒度MoE模型在推理时减少激活专家数量会因重新归一化导致性能大幅下降，而此前研究未区分"选择哪些专家"和"归一化强度"这两个不同效应。作者希望解耦这两者，找到仅通过调整归一化方式就能保持性能的同时减少计算开销的方法。

Method: 提出一种分离方案：激活前k₁个专家（决定推理计算量），但使用前k₂个专家的概率质量进行归一化（决定输出增益强度）。k₂无需任何参数、训练或额外计算开销。该设计将"专家选择"与"归一化参考集"解耦，允许在减少激活专家数的同时保持校准窗口不变。

Result: 在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B上，8→4专家时MMLU从4.65点损失降至0.35点（k₂=16）；在11倍更大的Qwen3.5-397B-A17B上，10→5专家仅损失0.55点。完全去除归一化会导致灾难性失败。同时发现perplexity与下游准确率偏好不同的k₂值，且专家身份比专家权重更重要，平衡/领域特化路由留给剪枝的空间有限。

Conclusion: MoE推理时的归一化参考质量是决定压缩性能的关键因素，保留合适的参考概率质量（k₂）可以在几乎不损失精度的情况下减半路由专家计算量。此外，perplexity不适合单独作为MoE压缩设置的选择指标，且目前的专家路由模式限制了通过剪枝进一步提升效率的可能性。

Abstract: Modern fine-grained Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models route each token to a small number of experts and renormalize their router probabilities. We show that this renormalization implicitly calibrates expert output gain to the training top-$k$: reducing $k$ at inference changes not only which experts are used but also the strength of the expert branch. We separate these effects by activating the top $k_1$ experts while normalizing by the probability mass of the top $k_2$ experts, introducing one integer with no parameters, training, or measurable compute overhead. On Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, reducing from 8 to 4 experts causes a 4.65-point MMLU drop under standard renormalization but only 0.35 points with $k_2=16$, while halving routed-expert compute. The result replicates on the $11\times$ larger Qwen3.5-397B-A17B, where reducing from 10 to 5 experts loses only 0.55 points with an appropriate reference set. Removing renormalization entirely is catastrophic, showing that preserving a suitable reference mass is crucial. We further find that perplexity and downstream accuracy favor different $k_2$, cautioning against selecting MoE compression settings using unlabeled text alone. Analyses also show that expert identity matters substantially more than expert weighting, while balanced and domain-specialized routing leaves limited room for expert pruning.

</details>


### [217] [Optimizer Memory Schedules for Outscaling the Overtraining Axis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04577)
*Katie Everett,Shikai Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了优化器在过度训练（overtraining）轴上的扩展行为，发现优化器的相对性能和最优超参数会随训练时长发生显著变化。在51M到253M参数的模型和1x到256x过度训练因子下，ADANA（带对数时间权重衰减和动量冷却）在长时间训练下优于AdamW，且缩放优势与DANA理论预测一致；而Muon和SOAP则提供相对恒定的token效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化器评估通常只在固定的训练预算下进行，忽略了训练时间轴的影响。但随着大规模模型越来越倾向于在固定数据量上长时间训练，理解优化器在不同训练时长下的缩放行为变得至关重要，这直接关系到优化器的选择与设计。

Method: 在51M到253M参数的模型上，以1x到256x的过度训练因子，系统比较AdamW、Muon、SOAP和ADANA四种优化器。在每个设置下扫描基础学习率，并单独调整各优化器在每种训练时长的固定记忆（fixed memory）超参数。对ADANA采用对数时间权重衰减和动量冷却策略。

Result: 发现：首选学习率调度在过度训练轴上可发生反转；最佳权重衰减系数近似按sqrt(OT)缩放；更长时间训练偏好更长的固定记忆。ADANA在每种训练时长单独调优AdamW的固定记忆后，其缩放优势依然存在。ADANA在对数时间权重衰减和动量冷却下，优势随训练时间累积，其缩放指数优势接近DANA理论在幂律随机特征上的预测。Muon和SOAP提供近似恒定的token效率优势，SOAP在最高过度训练因子下可能获得更多优势。ADANA初始落后于两个矩阵预条件优化器，但随着训练增加逐渐追赶，超过Muon，并在最高OT因子下与SOAP竞争。

Conclusion: 训练时长（过度训练范围）应作为优化器评估与设计的关键维度。不同优化器的相对优势和最佳超参数随训练时间显著变化，这对如何在长时间训练场景中选择和设计优化器具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: We investigate how optimizers scale across the overtraining axis and show that relative optimizer performance and optimal hyperparameters change substantially with training horizon. In particular, we study how matrix-preconditioned methods (Muon and SOAP) and a momentum-scheduled method (ADANA) scale relative to AdamW. We compare these four optimizers across models from 51M to 253M parameters and overtraining (OT) factors from 1x to 256x, sweeping the base learning rate at every setting. The preferred learning rate schedule can reverse across the overtraining axis, the best weight decay coefficient scales approximately as sqrt(OT), and longer horizons generally favor longer fixed memory. ADANA's scaling advantage over AdamW persists after tuning AdamW's fixed memory separately at each horizon. Log-time weight decay and momentum cooldown provide substantial gains for ADANA that compound as training increases. With this treatment, ADANA outscales AdamW with an exponent advantage close to that predicted by DANA theory on power-law random features. Muon and SOAP instead provide roughly constant token-efficiency advantages over AdamW across most of the measured range, although SOAP may gain further at the highest overtraining factors. ADANA begins behind both matrix-preconditioned optimizers but closes the gaps as training increases, surpassing Muon and becoming competitive with SOAP at our highest OT factors. These results establish training horizon as an essential axis for optimizer evaluation and design.

</details>


### [218] [Representation Redundancy and Structural Complexity in Finite-Field Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04583)
*Zheng Zhang,Na Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限域 F_{2^n} 上逆运算在不同有序 F_2-基表示下的布尔结构与学习难度变化。证明两个有序基诱导相同坐标逆映射当且仅当它们属于同一 Galois 轨道，基与逆映射的对应关系恰好是 n 对一。分析了三种布尔表述（参考型、混合表示型、完整原始型）的代数次数与 ANF leap 性质，并通过神经网络实验验证了学习难度排序与理论一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解数学运算的表示形式如何影响其代数形式和学习难度，特别是有限域求逆运算在不同基表示下的结构差异，为密码学中 S 盒等数学原语的设计与分析提供理论依据。

Method: 通过数学证明刻画有序基与逆映射之间的 Galois 轨道关系；对三种布尔表述进行代数次数和 ANF leap 的理论分析；使用穷举计算验证理论结果；通过多层感知机控制实验测试不同表示下的学习难度与 Galois 轨道冗余的泛化收益。

Result: 三种表述的代数次数分别为 n-1、2(n-1)、至多 3(n-1)，ANF leap 分别为 1、2、至少 n；穷举计算与理论一致，多层感知机实验显示学习难度排序与理论相符，Galois 轨道冗余仅提供有限的泛化收益。

Conclusion: 表示间的精确冗余可以与布尔结构和学习行为的变化共存，当表示被作为输入的一部分暴露时，即使数学上等价的表示也可能导致不同的学习表现。

Abstract: The representation chosen for a mathematical operation can affect both its algebraic form and its empirical learning difficulty. We study this phenomenon for inversion over \(\mathbb F_{2^n}\), with field elements expressed in varying ordered \(\mathbb F_2\)-bases. We prove that two ordered bases induce the same coordinate inversion map if and only if they belong to the same Galois orbit. Since every orbit has size \(n\), the correspondence between ordered bases and distinct inversion maps is exactly \(n\)-to-one. We then analyze three Boolean formulations of inversion. The reference formulation has algebraic degree \(n-1\) and joint ANF leap \(1\), the mixed representation formulation has degree \(2(n-1)\) and joint ANF leap \(2\), and the complete raw formulation has degree at most \(3(n-1)\) and joint ANF leap at least \(n\). Exhaustive computations agree with the theoretical results and bounds in the cases considered. Controlled experiments with multilayer perceptrons show the same ordering in learning difficulty, while Galois orbit redundancy provides only a limited generalization benefit under the tested conditions. These results show that exact redundancy among representations can coexist with changes in Boolean structure and learning behavior when the representation is exposed as part of the input.

</details>


### [219] [GNN-Guided Graph Coarsening and Adaptive QUBO Penalties for the Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows on a Quantum Annealer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04593)
*Youssef Kamel Rezk,Paweł Gora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究量子退火求解带时间窗的容量车辆路径问题（CVRPTW）时的图粗化方法，提出自适应罚则校准和基于图神经网络（GNN）的合并评分替代手工调参，在Solomon基准上显著提升可行性，并在D-Wave Advantage2上验证了条件化效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的车辆路径问题QUBO图粗化启发式方法需要针对不同问题族进行专属调参，在随机实例上不可靠，限制了量子退火求解CVRPTW的实际应用效果。

Method: 提出两个核心改进：（1）自适应罚则校准——移除非绑定约束、归一化绑定约束并缩放剩余罚则，控制内部系数范围，从而改善原始采样质量；（2）用GNN替换手工调参的合并评分函数，以单一配置适用于所有Solomon问题族。实验采用模拟退火和D-Wave Advantage2处理器进行验证。

Result: 罚则校准将平均原始约束违反数从33.0降至0.06（相同求解预算，p=3.7e-11）。GNN在N=10时实现所有Solomon问题族100%可行性（R型从80%提升至100%）；N=10-100范围可行性为83% vs 69%，85/90实例-规模对优于或持平；N=80,100时差异显著（p=0.002）。硬件实验中可行样本从0.02%提升至39%。

Conclusion: 罚则条件化（而非单纯的罚则规模或问题大小）是提升量子退火QUBO求解质量的关键因素；GNN可以替代手工调参的合并启发式并提供跨问题族的泛化能力；经典修复+局部搜索仍是端到端求解成本的参考基准。

Abstract: Graph coarsening reduces the large Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) formulations arising when vehicle-routing problems are solved by quantum annealing. Nearby customers with compatible time windows are merged into super-nodes, the reduced problem is solved, and the solution is expanded to the original graph. For the Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows (CVRPTW), existing coarsening heuristics require family-specific tuning and remain unreliable on random instances. We address these limitations on the Solomon benchmark using simulated annealing and a D-Wave Advantage2 processor.
  We first introduce adaptive penalty calibration. Uniform penalty scaling has little effect, whereas controlling the internal coefficient range substantially improves raw samples. Removing non-binding constraints, normalising binding ones, and scaling the remaining penalties reduces mean raw constraint violations from 33.0 to 0.06 at the same solver budget (p=3.7e-11, n=56). A variable-count-preserving control attributes this gain to conditioning rather than problem size.
  Second, we replace the hand-tuned merge score with a graph neural network (GNN) using one configuration across all families. At N=10, it achieves 100% feasibility across all Solomon families, including R-type (100% vs. 80% for the tuned heuristic). Across N=10,...,100, feasibility is 83% vs. 69%, with the GNN better or tied on 85/90 instance-size pairs. At N=80,100, the difference is significant (p=0.002; 25/25 pairs), while the QUBO remains approximately 5-6 times smaller.
  Finally, hardware experiments reproduce the conditioning effect at fixed logical variable count: feasible samples increase from 0.02% to 39% across 13 instances. Classical repair with local search remains a reference bound for end-to-end solution cost.

</details>


### [220] [Too Rare to Learn: Prescribed Cyclone Tracks Degrade a Bay of Bengal Ocean Emulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04635)
*Sumaiya Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究测试了在热带气旋暴露海域的神经海洋模拟器中，将气旋作为预设输入是否有助于预测，结果发现这种做法有害：在孟加拉湾的实验中，仅提供海洋数据的U-Net在所有测试中均优于持久性预测，而加入气旋通道的模型则全部劣于持久性预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经海洋模拟器正被提出用于气旋暴露沿海海域的区域预报，一个自然的设计选择是将气旋作为预设输入提供给网络，但该选择是否真的有益尚未得到验证。

Method: 从GLORYS12再分析数据中保留15个完整气旋（强度65至150节），比较两个除四个预设气旋轨迹通道外完全相同的U-Net模型，每种设置使用三个随机种子进行实验。

Result: 仅海洋模型在每次运行中均击败持久性预测，而气旋条件模型在每次运行中都输给持久性预测，两者技能范围不相交（p=3.1e-5）。原因在于暴露频率而非信号内容：气旋通道在训练数据中仅7.9%的天数非零，导致激活时处于分布外状态。推断时用无气旋图替代真实气旋图可使保留气旋的预报在每个种子中提升7.5%至16.4%。

Conclusion: 条件化网络学习了对罕见信号的响应，但这种响应是自信而错误的。直接向网络提供气旋作为预设输入可能不是有效的设计选择，需要在训练数据暴露频率或模型架构上做出调整。

Abstract: Neural ocean emulators are being proposed for regional forecasting in cyclone-exposed coastal seas, and a natural design choice is to hand the network the cyclone as a prescribed input. We test that choice in the Bay of Bengal and find it harmful. We withhold 15 whole cyclones spanning 65 to 150 kt from GLORYS12 reanalysis and compare two U-Nets that are identical except for four prescribed cyclone-track channels. Across three seeds the ocean-only model beats persistence in every run and the storm-conditioned model loses to it in every run, with the two skill ranges disjoint (p = 3.1e-5, paired across storms). The cause is exposure frequency rather than signal content: the channels are non-zero on only 7.9% of training days, so they are out of distribution the moment they activate. The extra error falls inside the prescribed storm footprint, and replacing the real cyclone map with a no-storm map at inference improves held-out storm forecasts by 7.5 to 16.4% in every seed. The conditioned network has learned a response to a rare signal that is confidently wrong.

</details>


### [221] [SMILE: Bridging Continuous Optimization and Discrete Symbolic Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04639)
*Mansooreh Montazerin,Antonio Ortega,Ajitesh Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SMILE框架，一种结合连续梯度优化与离散符号恢复的混合符号回归方法，通过结构分析、连续优化和符号恢复三个阶段，在SRBench基准上实现了最高噪声水平下的最高符号解率，并在精度与复杂度权衡上处于Pareto前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号回归方法在组合搜索空间中收敛缓慢，且缺乏利用数据中组合结构的机制，导致难以高效发现封闭形式的数学表达式。

Method: SMILE框架包含三个阶段：（1）结构分析：识别目标表达式的组合层次结构；（2）连续优化：使用可解释激活函数学习编码目标表达式的网络参数；（3）符号恢复：通过结构化剪枝、系数优化和舍入，将学习到的网络蒸馏为包含精确符号常量的紧凑表达式。

Result: 在SRBench上对ground-truth和黑盒数据集进行评估，SMILE在最大噪声水平下取得最高的符号解率，展现了对竞争方法性能大幅下降场景的强鲁棒性；在精度与复杂度Pareto前沿上表现一致，能以远少于竞争方法的时间恢复更简单的表达式。

Conclusion: SMILE通过统一连续优化和符号恢复，有效解决了符号回归中的组合搜索瓶颈，在鲁棒性和效率上均优于现有方法，并通过消融实验验证了各阶段的贡献。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) discovers closed-form mathematical expressions from data, offering interpretability beyond black-box models. Existing methods suffer from slow convergence in combinatorial search spaces and lack mechanisms to exploit compositional structure in the data. We introduce SMILE (Sine, Multiplication, Identity, Logarithm, Exponential), a hybrid framework that unifies continuous gradient-based optimization with discrete symbolic recovery through three stages: structural analysis of the data to identify the compositional hierarchy of the target expression, continuous optimization to learn parameters of a network that encodes the target expression using interpretable activations, and symbolic recovery through structured pruning, coefficient optimization, and rounding. This final stage distills the learned network into a compact expression with exact symbolic constants. We evaluate SMILE on SRBench across ground-truth and black-box datasets, with ablation studies validating each component. SMILE achieves the highest symbolic solution rate at the largest noise levels, demonstrating strong robustness where competing methods degrade substantially. It consistently lies on the Pareto front of accuracy versus complexity, recovering significantly simpler expressions in a fraction of the time required by the competing methods.

</details>


### [222] [Interpretability for Turing Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04661)
*Billy Snikkers,Rumi Salazar,Daniel Murfet,Will Troiani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了神经网络的敏感性（susceptibilities）可解释性技术能够通过探测嘈杂图灵机学习问题的局部损失景观，识别图灵机中存在的算法结构。作者证明算法中的对称性和路径分离会诱导敏感性矩阵中的置换对称性和低秩块，并在确定性有限自动机（DFA）上进行了实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否利用神经网络的可解释性技术来揭示算法结构，将神经网络的内部表征与图灵机实现的算法特征联系起来。

Method: 理论证明方面，展示图灵机算法的对称性和路径分离分别对应敏感性矩阵的置换对称性和低秩块结构。实证方面，在确定性有限自动机（DFA）数据集上计算敏感性矩阵，并通过主成分分析（PCA）和聚类方法恢复算法特征。

Result: 敏感性矩阵能够编码图灵机的算法结构；通过PCA和聚类可以在敏感性空间中有效恢复DFA的算法特征。

Conclusion: 敏感性技术为连接神经网络可解释性与算法结构分析提供了新的桥梁，可用于识别和恢复底层算法的结构特征。

Abstract: We show that susceptibilities, an interpretability technique developed for neural networks, can identify the presence of algorithmic structure in Turing machines by probing the local loss landscape of a learning problem for noisy Turing machines introduced by Murfet and Troiani (arXiv:2504.08075). We prove that symmetries and path separation in the algorithm implemented by a Turing machine induce permutation symmetries and low-rank blocks in its susceptibility matrix. We study this empirically on a set of deterministic finite automata (DFAs) and demonstrate that algorithmic features can be recovered by principal component analysis and clustering methods in susceptibility space.

</details>


### [223] [WEECFP-SuRGE: Wide Embedded Extended Connectivity Fingerprint with Substructure Rotary Graph-distance Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04672)
*Robert Epps*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WEECFP（一种参数无关的1024维连续分子指纹）和WEECFP-SuRGE（一种基于SuRGE图距离编码的Transformer架构）。该方法的7模型集成在TDC ADMET排行榜上取得平均回归排名最低，在没有外部预训练的方法中排名第一，并在多个基准上取得领先。此外，WEECFP tokenization几乎无损（99.9%的分子可精确重建），三参考最远优先编码与真实图距离相关性达r=0.901。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分子表示方法（如经典指纹和外部预训练模型）在ADMET预测等任务上存在精度瓶颈或依赖大规模外部预训练资源，作者希望设计一种无需外部预训练、参数友好且信息保留能力强的连续分子指纹及配套架构，以提升分子性质预测性能。
